### [1] [Towards a Risk Assessment of Malicious Skill Files in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05223)
*Rui Yang,Michael Fu,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Chetan Arora,Joey Chua*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 构建对抗性技能基准与可重复评估管线，实测显示企业级编码代理对伪装恶意技能高度脆弱，安全识别极低，需在部署前评估并缓解技能接口风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着有权限的自动编码代理在企业环境中普及，技能接口（以自然语言技能文件为载体）成为可供滥用的新攻击面；研究旨在量化该风险、提供基准并验证可重复的评估流程以推动防护措施研究。

Method: 采用六种LLM跨四个模型家族对471个真实shell命令进行对抗性技能合成，生成2,826个技能。构建评估管线：运行分层（run stratification）、证据锚定（evidence anchoring）、拒绝否决（refusal veto）、确定性声明意图覆盖（declared-intent override），并用三LLM裁判面板评估行为，结果用人工盲审金标准验证（Cohen's kappa=0.85）。在两个企业级代理上完成5,629次运行并统计利用率与安全识别率。

Result: 作者通过对抗性合成技能并构建评估管线，生成了一个包含2,826个伪装技能（源自471条真实shell命令，映射到11个MITRE ATT&CK战术）的基准数据集，并在5,629次运行中对两个企业级代理（Gemini CLI与Qwen Code）进行了大规模表征；结果显示代理被滥用的比率极高（Gemini CLI约95.5–96.1%，Qwen Code约71.6–74.0%），明确安全识别仅占约1.99%。作者公开了代码与数据集。

Conclusion: 技能接口显著扩大了代理的攻击面；企业在采用自动编码代理前必须评估技能文件风险、加强检测与访问控制。作者提供了可复现的数据与工具以支持后续研究与防护开发。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents are increasingly embedded in enterprise software workflows with delegated authority over connected systems. Central to this architecture is the agent skills interface: folders of instructions and scripts that agents load dynamically to specialize their behavior. This interface also widens the attack surface, letting malicious shell commands hide within natural-language skill files. We make three contributions. First, an adversarial skill-synthesis method using six LLMs across four families to transform 471 real-world shell commands into benign-appearing skills, released as a benchmark of 2,826 skills mapped to 11 MITRE ATT&CK tactics. Second, a reproducible evaluation pipeline coupling run stratification, evidence anchoring, a refusal veto, and a deterministic declared-intent override with a three-judge LLM-as-a-judge panel, validated against a blind human gold standard (Cohen's kappa = 0.85). Third, a large-scale characterization of two enterprise-grade agents across 5,629 completed runs. Gemini CLI is exploited in 95.5-96.1% of runs and Qwen Code in 71.6-74.0% (raw majority vote to declared-intent-corrected estimate, both within the human gold standard), nearly invariant to the generating model. Explicit safety recognition occurs in only 1.99% of runs. Enterprises must assess and mitigate skill-interface risk before adopting coding agents. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/awsm-research/AgentJailbreak

</details>


### [2] [Improving Debugging in Verification-Aware Languages Through Automated Fault Localization: A Case Study in Dafny](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05399)
*Álvaro Silva,Isabel Amaral,João Pascoal Faria,Alexandra Mendes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对 Dafny 的自动化故障定位研究表明，利用并优化验证器生成的反例（尤其是对反例进行结构化处理与聚合）比基于状态快照的方法更有效、更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dafny 等验证感知语言在验证失败时通常只报告触发断言的条件或单一路径的反例，开发者仍需手动分析长执行轨迹以定位根本错误，亟需自动化的故障定位技术。

Method: 实现并比较两类定位策略：1) 状态式：仿效 AutoFix，通过推断不变量与谓词来识别可疑状态；2) 反例式：包含从原始反例提取、对单条反例进行结构化排序到对多条反例进行聚合的一系列技术。使用 MutDafny 在 DafnyBench 上生成变异体数据集，并以 EXAM 分数衡量定位效果。

Result: 实验证明：反例式方法在定位效果上明显优于状态式；在反例式内部，结构化排名带来最大的改进，多反例聚合进一步提升覆盖与稳健性，从而减少路径偏倚并提高调试实用性。

Conclusion: 在验证感知语言中，基于反例（counterexample-based）的定位方法显著优于基于程序状态（state-based）的“快照”式定位；在反例方法中，对单条反例进行结构化排序提升最大，多条反例聚合进一步增强鲁棒性并降低解算器引入的路径偏倚。

Abstract: Verification-aware languages, like Dafny, integrate formal specifications directly into source code to enable static correctness checks. However, when verification fails, the feedback provided is often limited to the specific condition of the error, such as a violated postcondition, rather than the root cause of the fault. While Dafny's counterexample features provide concrete execution traces, these typically expose a single failing path per assertion failure, leaving the developer to manually look through the entire trace to locate the error.
  This paper investigates automated fault localization for verification-aware languages by comparing two paradigms: state-based and counterexample-based localization. Our state-based localization strategy replicates the ``snapshot'' methodology of AutoFix by inferring invariants and predicates to identify suspicious program states. The counterexample-based strategy consists of a family of techniques that progressively enrich the use of verifier output: from raw counterexample extraction, to structured single-trace ranking, and to multi-trace aggregation.
  To validate these methods, we present an evaluation framework using MutDafny to generate a diverse mutant dataset from DafnyBench and measure localization effectiveness using the EXAM score. Our results show that counterexample-based approaches substantially outperform state-based localization in this setting. Structured ranking over a single trace yields the largest improvement over raw counterexample output, while multi-trace aggregation provides additional gains in robustness and debugging utility by increasing coverage and reducing path bias introduced by the solver. These findings demonstrate that effective fault localization in verification-aware languages depends both on using counterexample information, and how that information is structured and diversified.

</details>


### [3] [Reasoning from Traces: Divergence-Guided Agentic Repair of WebAssembly Discrepancies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05521)
*Liyan Huang,Kaicheng Wang,Weihang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: WasmMend通过先定位运行时分歧再用LLM生成补丁，能显著提高修复Native与Wasm功能性差异的成功率（70% vs ≈50%基线）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨平台复用C/C++代码为Wasm承诺可移植、快速且沙箱化的二进制，但实践中编译器或平台库差异会导致Wasm与原生行为不一致。这些差异源于平台运行时层面、难以从源代码直接发现，导致传统和LLM修复方法效果有限。

Method: 两阶段流程：1) 差分追踪分析（differential trace analysis）定位首次发生分歧的函数；2) 在定位指引下，LLM驱动的推理代理分析根因并生成补丁以消除分歧。

Result: 在真实C/C++项目上的实验显示WasmMend修复率为70.0%，优于无聚焦的agent基线（50.2%）和带运行时LLM插桩的改进方法（54.5%），表明基于分歧定位的推理能显著提升跨平台修复效果。

Conclusion: WasmMend通过聚焦差异化回溯与基于LLM的补丁生成，有效修复了Native与Wasm之间的功能性差异，从而在实测C/C++项目上显著提升修复成功率。

Abstract: WebAssembly (Wasm) promises seamless reuse of C/C++ codebases as portable, fast, sandboxed binaries. In practice, however, this promise often falls short: recent studies show that cross-compiling the same C/C++ source to Wasm and native binaries frequently leads to runtime discrepancies, owing to library implementation differences or compiler bugs. Since the root causes lie in the platform-level runtime and are hidden beneath the source code, even state-of-the-art LLM-based repair agents often fail to fix these discrepancies. In this paper, we present WasmMend, the first system to automatically repair Native-Wasm functional discrepancies. WasmMend converts the undirected exploration to a focused reasoning task in two stages: First, a novel differential trace analysis approach localizes the function where Wasm and native executions initially diverge; guided by this localization, LLM agents then reason about the root causes and generate patches that eliminate the divergent behavior. Experiments on real-world C/C++ projects show that WasmMend achieves a fix rate of 70.0%, compared to 50.2% for the agentic baseline and 54.5\% for the approach augmented with repair-time LLM-based instrumentation, demonstrating the value of divergence-guided reasoning for cross-platform repair.

</details>


### [4] [Exploring Dependence, Overreliance, and Addiction Related Behaviors Associated with Large Language Model Use Among Software Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05561)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Italo Santos,Matheus de Moraes Leça,Cleyton Magalhaes,Mairieli Wessel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLM已成为软件工程日常工具，使用大多属功能性但存在过度依赖风险；应通过培训、工具设计与流程保障来维持适当依赖与验证实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估在职业软件开发中，工程师使用LLM时是否出现依赖、过度依赖或与成瘾相关的行为模式及其体验。

Method: 对119名软件从业人员开展探索性问卷调查，使用描述性统计和参与者开放式回答的质性主题分析。

Result: 受访者主要报告功能性依赖（将LLM整合入常规工作以提高生产力/效率）；有过度依赖的模式（优先LLM而非文档/同事），但通常仍会验证输出；成瘾样行为较少，主要表现为难以控制使用或情感依附，而非明显的功能受损。

Conclusion: LLMs已成为职业软件工程实践的习惯性工具，主要呈现功能性依赖；少量证据显示过度依赖（优先使用LLM而非查文档/询同事），与‘成瘾’相关的受控受损证据较少。需通过信任校准、职业判断与持续验证来促进适当依赖。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) has changed how software engineers perform everyday development activities. While these systems provide substantial support for tasks such as code generation, debugging, and documentation, their increasing integration into professional workflows has also raised questions regarding developers' reliance on these tools and the emergence of dependence, overreliance, and addiction-related behaviors. This study investigates how software engineers experience the use of LLMs during professional software development, with attention to behavioral patterns associated with dependence, overreliance, and addiction-related behaviors. An exploratory survey was conducted with 119 software practitioners. The data were analyzed using descriptive statistics and qualitative thematic analysis of participants' open-ended responses. Participants primarily described functional dependence, with LLMs becoming integrated into routine software engineering activities because of the productivity and efficiency they provide. Responses also suggested patterns consistent with overreliance, particularly through prioritizing LLMs over documentation or peer consultation while continuing to verify generated outputs. Reports associated with addiction-related behaviors were less common and primarily reflected difficulty moderating use or emotional attachment to the technology rather than impaired control. The findings suggest that LLMs are becoming a habitual component of professional software engineering practice. While most reported use appears functional, the results indicate the importance of promoting appropriate reliance by supporting trust calibration, professional judgment, and verification throughout software development.

</details>


### [5] [JTA: Joint Testability Architecture for Scenario-Based Validation of Safety-Critical Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05594)
*Wenyao Xue,Jiandi Wang,Yichen Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Validation adequacy in safety-critical software depends on more than the system under test. Critical scenarios must be constructed under controlled conditions, execution evidence must be aligned into verdict-ready form, and abnormal outcomes must be attributable to actionable causes. Existing testability research remains largely artifact-centric and offers little architectural support for reasoning about the combined capability of the scenario, the test system, and the system under test. Joint Testability Architecture (JTA) addresses this gap by treating those three elements as a single object of analysis and design. It characterizes validation capability along three dimensions--controllability, observability, and isolability--and organizes them through three domains, three bridges, and an analysis-design-evaluation-refinement loop. JTA also introduces scenario contracts, joint capability assessment, validation blind-spot identification, and bridge-oriented design actions that map capability gaps to concrete improvements in control points, evidence organization, and attribution boundaries. An illustrative analysis of ArduPilot failsafe validation shows that link-loss scenarios are comparatively mature, whereas state-estimation anomaly scenarios remain harder to validate because evidence alignment and attribution semantics are weaker. JTA is not a replacement for existing testing or safety-analysis techniques; it provides an architectural basis for modeling, designing, and assessing scenario-based validation in safety-critical software.

</details>


### [6] [Towards Competence-Based Management for Open Source Software Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05599)
*Sabahat Younas,Márcia Moraes,Fabio Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 用代码级度量构建能力评估与任务所需能力预测模型，从而帮助替补核心贡献者；复现包已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着核心贡献者流失风险上升，手动寻找与评估替补成本高且信息不充分。现有工作对能力衡量深度不足，作者通过自动化的代码度量与模型化方法希望提供可量化、可操作的能力评估以支持替换与培养决策。

Method: 收集贡献者历史贡献（代码提交/修改），提取代码度量（如复杂度、行数、提交频率、变更范围、模块熟悉度等），构建能力标签（可能为等级或数值），训练监督学习模型进行能力预测，并用常见分类/回归指标评估模型性能。

Result: 研究通过从贡献者的源码贡献中收集代码度量并训练监督模型来评估贡献者能力与预测完成特定任务所需的能力等级，旨在识别并培训替补核心贡献者；提供了复现包。

Conclusion: 基于源码的度量与机器学习模型在一定程度上能够量化并预测贡献者解决任务的能力，有助于快速识别候选人并制定培训策略，但存在度量覆盖不全、标签噪声、时间与项目迁移性等限制，需结合社交与质量指标并做严格验证。

Abstract: Contributors to Open Source Software (OSS) projects are vital to maintaining the health of both communities and projects. However, the number of projects experiencing core contributors' disengagement has increased to the point that it risks the projects' survival. Finding new contributors to replace the workforce is challenging and time-intensive due to several factors, including a lack of precise knowledge about potential candidates' competences, which may need to be confirmed through interviews and exams. Previous studies provided indications of the contributor's competences, although they lack depth in understanding competence levels, which can result in poor knowledge about the contributor's capabilities. To address this gap, we assess contributors' competence by collecting code metrics related to source code from contributions. We also propose a competence model able to predict the competence level required to solve tasks. By properly assessing contributors' competences, we can identify and train candidates to replace core contributors. Our replication package, including code, data, and documentation, is available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21605122

</details>


### [7] [Keeping Models and Code in Sync: Roundtrip Engineering for Tactical Domain-Driven Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05612)
*Weixing Zhang,Mario Herb,Wai Chung Dorothy Cheng,Michael Wagner,Bowen Jiang,Tianhai Liu,Anne Koziolek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出并实现了一个基于元模型的双向 DDD 同步工具（JDomInO），正向生成已在完整场景中验证，逆向映射单元测试通过，但需要更多端到端、跨项目及行为层面验证以证实可用性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDD 的词汇和模型能帮助团队管理复杂业务逻辑，但代码与模型经常发生漂移。设计工具链以在系统演化中保持模型与代码同步，从而维持一致的共享语言和语义边界。

Method: 基于共享元模型实现双向同步：正向路径从领域模型确定性生成 Java 代码结构；逆向路径从现有 Java 代码重建领域模型。实现涵盖战术 DDD 的 12 种构建块，正向在 Hotel Management 场景上完整验证，逆向映射通过单元测试，端到端验证进行中。

Result: 实现了一个名为 JDomInO 的工具链，确定性地生成代码结构并实现了逆向映射逻辑；在一个完整场景中完成正向验证，逆向映射有单元测试，提出了将结构化领域模型作为 AI 代码助理精确上下文层的应用设想。

Conclusion: JDomInO 有望成为保持领域模型与 Java 代码库一致性的实用工具，但目前验证仍不充分，逆向路径的端到端效果与工程适用性需要更多案例与量化评估。

Abstract: Domain-Driven Design gives teams a shared vocabulary for complex business logic, but that vocabulary only stays useful as long as the model and the code agree with each other. In practice, they drift apart: code changes outpace the model, or model revisions never make it into the codebase. This paper presents JDomInO, a bidirectional synchronization toolchain for tactical DDD that keeps a Java codebase and its domain model connected through a shared metamodel, with the goal of keeping the two in sync as the system evolves. JDomInO generates Java code structure deterministically from a domain model (forward path) and reconstructs a domain model from existing Java code (reverse path). The forward path has been fully validated on a Hotel Management scenario covering all 12 building block types in the metamodel; the reverse path's mapping logic has passed unit testing, with end-to-end validation underway. We also outline how the structured domain model produced by JDomInO could serve as a precision context layer for AI code assistants, helping them respect aggregate boundaries and DDD semantics that raw source code alone does not convey.

</details>


### [8] [Agent-Based Test Assertion Generation via Diverse Perspective Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05822)
*Dong Wang,Qiaoyu Han,Lin Yang,Jianyi Zhou,Guangtai Liang,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AssertMate improves LLM-based unit-test assertion generation by combining static-target identification, multi-agent expected-value prediction (code-gen, RAG, CoT), and an LLM judge to select the best assertion, yielding substantial gains on Defects4J and EvoSuite.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing methods (e.g., ChatAssert) have limited accuracy, over-rely on oversampling, and are sensitive to model randomness due to one-shot prompting. The work aims to improve quality and reliability of LLM-generated assertions by aggregating diverse generation perspectives and using a selection judge.

Method: AssertMate: an agent-based framework for assertion generation composed of (1) actual value construction via static analysis and type-aware heuristics to identify assertion targets; (2) multi-perspective expected value prediction using separate agents for code generation, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning; (3) an LLM-as-a-Judge mechanism that adjudicates and selects the best assertion from candidates.

Result: On Defects4J, AssertMate achieves significantly better compilation success and test pass rates, higher bug detection, and outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Integration with EvoSuite yields improved mutation coverage and kill counts. Ablation studies confirm each component contributes meaningfully and complementarily.

Conclusion: Aggregating multiple complementary perspectives and using a judge mechanism yields more accurate, reliable, and useful LLM-generated assertions; each component is necessary and contributes to overall effectiveness.

Abstract: Test assertions are critical elements of unit tests, serving as checkpoints to validate expected behavior and ensure software correctness. Numerous techniques have been proposed to automate assertion generation, with recent progress notably driven by large language models (LLMs). Despite the promise, existing approaches such as ChatAssert suffer from modest accuracy, heavy reliance on oversampling, and vulnerability to model randomness due to one-shot prompting. To address these limitations, we propose AssertMate, a novel agent-based assertion generation framework that enhances the quality and reliability of LLM-generated assertions through three key components: (1) actual value construction that identifies assertion targets via static analysis and type-aware heuristics; (2) multi-perspective expected value prediction using code generation, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning agents; and (3) an LLM-as-a-Judge collaboration mechanism to select the most appropriate assertion. Evaluation on the Defects4J benchmark demonstrates that AssertMate significantly outperforms state-of-the-art techniques in compilation success and pass rates, along with substantially higher bug detection capabilities. Integration with EvoSuite further validates AssertMate's practicality, yielding superior mutation coverage and kill counts. Ablation studies reveal that each of the three components makes a significant and complementary contribution to the overall performance. This work affirms the great potential of aggregating diverse perspectives to enhance the effectiveness of LLM-based assertion generation.

</details>


### [9] [CodeGrep: An RL-Trained Retrieval Agent for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05886)
*Wuya Chen,Yihao yang,Yang Cao,Yue Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出CodeGrep：一个14B的检索代理，用GRPO端到端训练，发起多回合并行的grep/glob/read工具调用，向冻结的下游编程代理返回候选文件，从而减少代理在仓库定位文件上的token开销并提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到大型编程代理在仓库探索上浪费大量token（grep/glob/view_file调用频繁），因此提出专门的检索代理以把探测成本前置、减少下游编码代理的探索token开销，从而提高整体效率。

Method: 从67K开源代理轨迹用CATM挖掘监督信号，构建Git-worktree多回合RL环境，用GRPO训练一个14B检索器以并行发出grep/glob/read调用，输出候选文件供冻结下游编码代理使用；在训练中将效率信号施加于advantage层以减少KL漂移。

Result: 在500个SWE-Bench实例上Preserve或提升解析率，并在解析实例上减少15%回合和19%token；同时比较BM25、Jina和CodeGrep的检索精度/下游效用，显示存在精度阈值，低于该阈值的检索器会降低性能。

Conclusion: CodeGrep在SWE-Bench Verified上的总体解析率不降反升（27.0% vs 25.8%），在已解析实例上显著减少回合数（-15%）和token数（-19%），并通过高精度检索（precision≈0.677）跨越了降低rollout成本的阈值。

Abstract: Modern LLM coding agents such as Claude Code and OpenHands share a common inefficiency: they spend much of their token budget finding the file to patch, rather than patching it. On SWE-Bench Verified, a 30B OpenHands agent averages 23 rounds and 631K tokens per resolved issue, with many calls spent on grep, glob, and view_file during repository exploration. We introduce CodeGrep, a 14B retrieval agent trained end-to-end with GRPO to issue multi-turn parallel grep, glob, and read tool calls and return candidate files to a frozen downstream coding agent.
  On all 500 SWE-Bench Verified instances, CodeGrep preserves resolve rate while substantially improving efficiency: 27.0% versus 25.8% for the no-retrieval baseline, with 15% fewer rounds and 19% fewer tokens on resolved instances. Across retrievers, downstream utility follows a precision threshold: BM25 with precision 0.375 degrades the agent, Jina with precision 0.445 is neutral, and CodeGrep with precision 0.677 crosses the threshold at which retrieval begins to reduce rollout cost. To enable this study, we mine supervision from 67K open-source agent trajectories using CATM and build a Git-worktree environment for multi-turn agent RL. In our setting, applying the efficiency signal at the advantage layer rather than the reward layer reduces KL drift and translates cleanly into downstream efficiency. We will release the model, training pipeline, RL environment, and evaluation harnesses.

</details>


### [10] [Mapping the Emerging Curriculum for AI-Assisted Software Engineering via Syllabus Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05898)
*Francis Geng,Anshul Shah,Mia Chen,Paul Denny,Juho Leinonen,Bill Griswold,Gerald Soosai Raj,Leo Porter*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对23门生成式AI软件工程课程大纲的迭代质性编码揭示了主题、目标、评估与工具记录的共性与差异，为该新兴课程领域的定义与未来课程设计提供实证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具正在重塑专业软件开发流程，大学需开设课程以培养学生在AI辅助开发环境中的能力；通过系统化分析现有课程大纲，可以了解该课程领域如何被定义并为后续课程设计提供经验基准。

Method: 收集并筛选23份公开可得的、上级别（upper-division）、计学分且明确涵盖生成式AI在软件工程中应用的课程大纲与教材；通过迭代的质性编码过程，对课程的学习目标、评估方式、主题目录和所记录的AI工具进行分类与汇总分析。

Result: 系统化地归纳出课程的常见学习目标（例如模型使用、 prompt 工程、评估与伦理）、常见评估形式（作业、项目、考试等）、主题分布（基础知识到实战应用）以及被记录的具体AI工具；并识别出课程在工具依赖性、伦理与可重复性等方面的差异，进而提出对研究者与教育者有用的建议。

Conclusion: 通过对23份面向生成式AI软件工程课程的大纲与教材的质性编码分析，论文得出这些课程在学习目标、评估方式、主题覆盖和工具记录上存在共性与差异，提供了关于该新兴课程领域如何被界定的实证证据，并为未来课程设计和研究提供了指导性见解。

Abstract: As Generative AI coding tools reshape professional software development, universities have begun designing courses to prepare students for AI-assisted development workflows. By analyzing the syllabi of these courses, we can gather empirical evidence about these courses, reveal how this emerging curricular area is being defined, and gain guidance for future curriculum design. We analyzed 23 publicly available syllabi and course materials of upper-division, credit-bearing courses that meet specific criteria, including explicitly addressing Generative AI in software engineering. Through iterative qualitative coding, we characterized courses' learning objectives, assessments, topics, and documented AI tools. Our analysis reveals commonalities and differences among these courses that allow researchers and educators to study and develop future courses.

</details>


### [11] [Escaping the Self-Repair Trap: Improving Test Oracle Generation via Dual-Context Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05917)
*Kefan Li,Hongyue Yu,Yuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DCAware通过高质量的上下文构建（结构化静态信息 + 有选择的动态状态检索）替代迭代自修复，避免Self-Repair Trap，既提高了断言的故障暴露能力，又降低了计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前以执行成功为目标的迭代自我修复方法，在生成回归测试断言（regression-oracle）时常常偏离真实目标：优化可执行性作为代理目标会导致产生容易满足但低效的断言，降低故障暴露能力。需要一种避免反馈驱动退化(Self-Repair Trap)、更侧重于高信噪比上下文支撑的方案。

Method: 设计一个计算高效的、非迭代的生成流程：先构建结构化静态上下文（如函数签名、注释、变更摘要等），再根据需要有选择地检索并注入运行时状态（例如关键变量、最近失败的堆栈信息），将两类信息喂给LLM以生成断言；省略多轮执行—反馈—修复循环，从而避免优化执行成功所致的偏差。实验以执行成功率和基于变异测试的故障揭示指标进行评估，并与若干基线方法比较。

Result: 提出非迭代的DCAware框架，通过将结构化静态上下文与选择性检索的动态状态结合，优先保证上下文质量，从而在不依赖多轮修复的情况下显著提升故障暴露效果，同时维持较高的执行成功率，并以更低计算成本优于现有方法。基于执行测试与变异测试的广泛实验验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 在回归断言生成场景中，提高上下文质量比引入复杂的迭代修复机制更能提升断言的故障揭示能力。DCAware提供了一条更高效、鲁棒的替代路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for regression-oracle completion, where a test prefix is given and the current program version is treated as expected behavior. Recent approaches increasingly rely on iterative self-repair and execution feedback, but optimizing execution success does not necessarily yield strong fault-revealing oracles. This objective, widely adopted in repair-based methods, serves only as a proxy and may be misaligned with the true goal of oracle generation. Such misalignment biases the repair process, giving rise to a feedback-driven degeneration that we term the Self-Repair Trap, where iterative repair progressively drives models toward assertions that are easier to satisfy but less effective at detecting faults. To address this issue, we propose DCAware, a computationally efficient, non-iterative framework that prioritizes high signal-to-noise contextual grounding over multi-round repair. DCAware integrates structured static context with selectively retrieved dynamic states, enabling precise and robust oracle generation without iterative feedback loops. Extensive experiments based on execution and mutation testing show that DCAware consistently improves fault-revealing effectiveness while maintaining high execution success, outperforming prior methods with substantially lower computational cost. Our results suggest that improving contextual quality is more effective than adding iterative repair complexity in the studied regression-oracle setting.

</details>


### [12] [Expertise-Based Developer Assignment for Long-Term Software Components in Open-Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05919)
*Faheem Ullah,Babar Shah,William Shanks,Ayesha Mohsin,Abrar Ullah*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出基于历史提交的开发者专长建模与自动分配web应用，实验显示缓存能显著加速，推荐准确度中等（72.4% Top-10），需补充评估细节和可扩展性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动分配工作多针对具体短期任务，缺乏针对新项目中需要长期专长组件的开发者分配方法；正确分配可提高效率与代码可维护性。

Method: 实现了一个web应用，基于开发者的历史Git提交构建专长模型（未指明具体特征或学习算法），用于在新项目中为任务自动匹配最优开发者；评测包含响应时间（不同后端模型）和命中率（目标开发者在Top-10中的占比）。

Result: 服务器端数据缓存将最坏情况速度提升约9.86倍；在被测任务中，72.4%的任务其目标开发者在候选47人中的Top-10内；但模型性能、比较基线和统计显著性等细节未充分报告。

Conclusion: 该系统通过基于历史Git提交建模开发者专长并自动分配任务，证明在推荐合适开发者方面可行，但存在性能与准确度限制，需要更多评估和改进。

Abstract: Open-source software development through GitHub has enabled countless software projects to be developed by developers from across the world. Assigning the right task to the right developer enables teams to work efficiently. Assigning a task which is not representative of a developer's expertise results in slower completion time and less maintainable code. Whilst much work has been done to automate this assignment for specific jobs, there is little work addressing the assignment of developers to expertise-specific long-term components of a new project. This paper produces a web application that models developers' expertise based on their previous Git commits and automatically assigns them to an optimal task within a new project. Testing showed that the system's speed varies depending on the back-end model used but server-side data caching improved worst-case speeds by a factor of 9.86. 72.4% of tasks had their target developer listed in the top 10, out of a possible 47.

</details>


### [13] [Sensor-Level Fault Diagnosis for Automotive Software Validation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05921)
*Mohammad Abboush,Hamza Ouarrad,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究提出在HIL平台上利用微调的开源指令型大模型（4-bit LoRA）对传感器信号的文本描述进行故障检测与诊断，以减少人工审查量并提高可解释性。方法为两阶段：先在dSPACE实时平台上自动执行需求检测定位违规录波，再将违规窗口的统计/关系/上下文描述嵌入固定提示，并用小模型映射到故障位置及理由。实验在汽油发动机6类注入故障上，4个模型族（2–8B参数）微调并测试，最小模型达81.6%准确率，表明适应性收敛比参数量更关键，且全部训练可在单块通用加速器上完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HIL验证产生大量多变量传感器数据，人工审查无法规模化；阈值方法缺乏定位与诊断信息，数据驱动黑盒分类器需要大量标注且不利于ISO 26262的可追溯性。挑战是实现数据高效、可解释且可追溯的故障诊断工具以嵌入验证流程。

Method: 两阶段框架：1）在dSPACE实时平台上进行自动化需求检查，筛选出违反安全需求的录波；2）对违例录波按滑动窗口提取统计、关系与上下文描述，构造固定文本提示输入至采用4-bit低秩适配（LoRA）微调的指令型开源LLM，模型输出故障位置并给出书面理由。对4个模型族（2B–8B参数）在汽油发动机6类故障上比较，评估准确率与收敛性，所有适配与评估在单块通用加速器上完成。

Result: 在汽油发动机案例上，最小模型（约2B？）在任务特定微调后达到与最大模型相同的81.6%准确率；另一个同规模模型未收敛，表明收敛性是关键因素。整个微调与评估流程能在单一商用加速器上运行，展示了资源可行性和任务有效性。

Conclusion: 指令型开源LLM经任务特定微调后，能在HIL验证环节提供数据高效、可解释的故障定位与诊断，减少人工负担并兼顾ISO 26262可追溯性；诊断性能受收敛性影响大于模型体量，且适配流程对计算资源要求较低。

Abstract: The pre-series validation of automotive software on hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) platforms produces large volumes of multivariate sensor recordings whose assessment against functional safety requirements exceeds what manual review can sustain at campaign scale. Threshold-based tooling reports that a deviation has occurred but neither identifies its nature nor locates its source, while data-driven classifiers, although accurate, rely on large labelled datasets and return opaque decisions that sit uneasily with the traceability demanded by ISO 26262. This study examines whether open-source instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs), given a textual description of sensor behaviour, can serve as data-efficient and interpretable engines for fault detection and diagnosis inside the validation loop. A two-phase framework is proposed: automated requirement checking on a dSPACE real-time platform first isolates the recordings that violate a safety requirement, and only these are inspected, with sliding windows of the signals reduced to statistical, relational, and contextual descriptors, embedded in a fixed prompt, and mapped by a 4-bit low-rank-adapted LLM to a fault location accompanied by a written justification. Four model families ranging from two to eight billion parameters were adapted and tested on a gasoline-engine case study spanning six injected fault classes. The smallest model matched the largest at 81.6\% accuracy, whereas a comparably sized model failed to converge, indicating that diagnostic competence under task-specific adaptation follows convergence rather than parameter count, with the entire adapt-and-evaluate cycle fitting on a single commodity accelerator.

</details>


### [14] [AgentExecutor: Partial Code Execution via Agentic Context Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05959)
*Junkai Chen,Chengran Yang,Xing Hu,Zhenhao Li,Xin Xia,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出AgentExecutor：基于多智能体的片段代码执行框架，通过三阶段流程（环境准备、动态探索与迭代修正、基于程序合成的前缀演化）利用LLM代理的循环思考—行动—反馈能力，扩展操作空间并采用自适应优化（覆盖率引导的上下文剪枝、前缀演化）以提升片段代码执行率与效率。实验在Stack Overflow片段和开源项目上显著优于Treefix，执行覆盖率、时间与成本均有大幅改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部分执行方法（如LExecutor、Treefix）受限于动作空间窄、反馈信息弱和固定的优化策略，导致推断上下文与修复依赖的能力不足、效率低下，需要更灵活、可扩展的自动化执行框架。

Method: 三阶段设计：1) 执行环境准备（创建资源文件、配置环境等）；2) 动态探索与迭代修正（LLM代理做出操作、运行并用反馈修正）；3) 前缀演化通过程序合成持续优化上下文；采用覆盖率引导的上下文剪枝与自适应优化策略。

Result: 在两套数据集上，AgentExecutor分别达到了约94%和90%代码覆盖率，较Treefix提升约19.9%和13.8%；执行时间最多减少80.3%，成本最多减少56.6%。

Conclusion: AgentExecutor通过多智能体协作与自适应优化，能更全面地推断缺失上下文与环境依赖，显著提升片段代码的可执行性和执行效率。

Abstract: Executing code snippets is essential for dynamic program analysis, but it remains challenging to execute an arbitrary code snippet due to issues like missing context and incomplete dependencies. Existing approaches to partial code execution, such as LExecutor and Treefix, leverage the power of language models to infer missing information and enable execution. However, they suffer from (i) limited action spaces and feedback, and (ii) rigid optimization strategies, which restrict their effectiveness and efficiency. In this paper, we propose AgentExecutor, a novel multi-agent framework for partial code execution. Our approach introduces a three-phase design: execution environment preparation, dynamic exploration with iterative refinement, and prefix evolution via program synthesis. Supported by the power of LLM agents who can think, act, and get feedback iteratively, AgentExecutor is able to autonomously explore a richer action space, enabling diverse operations such as creating resource files and resolving environment configuration. Furthermore, it adopts adaptive optimization strategies, including coverage-guided context pruning and prefix evolution via program synthesis, to systematically improve the execution quality of partial code. We evaluate AgentExecutor on two widely used datasets comprising Stack Overflow snippets and open-source project code. The results show that AgentExecutor achieves up to 94% and 90% code coverage, outperforming the state-of-the-art approach Treefix by 19.9% and 13.8%, respectively. In addition, AgentExecutor significantly reduces execution time (by up to 80.3%) and cost (by up to 56.6%). These findings demonstrate that AgentExecutor provides an effective and efficient solution for partial code execution.

</details>


### [15] [LangChoiceBench: Measuring and Explaining Programming-Language Choice in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06041)
*Lukas Twist,Twm Stone,Helen Yannakoudakis,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LangChoiceBench reveals pervasive, often unjustified Python bias and inconsistent behavior in LLM project-level language selection, with hallucinated or contradictory rationales in reasoning traces.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: There is no systematic way to measure LLMs' language-selection behavior (particularly Python preference) at project scale; the benchmark aims to quantify bias, consistency, and diversity across models.

Method: Introduced LangChoiceBench, a benchmark of 28 projects across seven software areas where Python is often a poor default; evaluated 25 diverse LLMs for language choice, recommendation-implementation consistency, and language diversity; analyzed 9,826 model reasoning traces to categorize motivations behind language choices.

Result: Findings: heavy over-selection of Python, low consistency between stated recommendation and generated implementation, smaller open-weight models more biased toward Python and less diverse in languages, and a notable fraction of cases where models fabricate or contradict reasoning (‘phantom evidence’).

Conclusion: LLMs exhibit a strong, unjustified bias toward selecting Python for project-level code generation; recommendation-implementation consistency is low; smaller/open-weight models show stronger Python preference and lower language diversity; models sometimes fabricate context (“phantom evidence”) or contradict their own reasoning.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been shown to exhibit strong Python preferences when generating project-level code, but there is currently no systematic way to measure this behaviour across new models. To bridge this gap, we introduce LangChoiceBench, a project-level code-generation benchmark for measuring Python preference, recommendation-implementation consistency, and language diversity. LangChoiceBench covers 28 projects across seven software areas where Python is often a poor default. We evaluate 25 diverse LLMs and find that Python remains heavily over-selected, recommendation-implementation consistency is low, and smaller open-weight models generally show stronger Python preference and lower language diversity. We further analyse 9,826 reasoning traces and find that most Python choices are automatic or driven primarily by ease, rather than explicit consideration of project requirements. In a smaller but important set of cases, models fabricate contextual support for choosing Python - a failure mode we call phantom evidence - or produce code that contradicts the language selected in their own reasoning.

</details>


### [16] [In Terms of Explainability: Refining Requirements for Self-Explainable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06049)
*Arno Leue,Akhila Bairy,Maike Schwammberger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过分析并整合现有定义，本文提出了统一的可解释性定义与结构化需求（分类法），并主张将解释的‘良好性/正确性’纳入定义，以推动形成正式标准和认证路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Autonomous and software-intensive systems are increasing in complexity and responsibility; existing definitions of explainability are inconsistent; regulatory and standardization needs (e.g., EU AI Act, IEEE 7001) motivate a unified set of definitions and requirements.

Method: 文献与标准分析：梳理现有定义与标准（如EU AI Act、IEEE 7001），合并分析结果以形成统一定义，构建结构化需求列表与分类法，并基于该分类提出将解释良好性纳入定义的论证；文中未报告实证检验。

Result: A set of unified definitions for explainability and (self-)explainable systems; a structured set of explainability requirements (a taxonomy); recommendation to incorporate explanation goodness/correctness into the definitions; argued support for formal standards, certification, and auditing.

Conclusion: 建立统一的可解释性分类法和需求有助于制定标准与审计流程；解释质量应成为定义的一部分；但需要后续在度量、验证与实际系统中的应用上开展工作。

Abstract: Autonomous and software-intensive systems have been growing in occurrence, complexity, and assumed responsibility. Due to the high complexity of these systems, properties like transparency and explainability must be a focus of investigation. To date, no universally applicable definition and guide for the development of (self-)explainable systems exists. A need for explainability standards has already been recognized in the EU AI Act and the IEEE Transparency Standard 7001-2021. To address this need, we propose unified definitions in explainability based on an analysis and combination of existing definitions. Additionally, we present structured explainability requirements that are necessary to build (self-)explainable systems. By analysing the resulting taxonomy, we propose the incorporation of explanation goodness and thus correctness of explanations into the unified definitions. With our approach, we support the development of formal standards for (self-)explainable systems. Establishing such a uniform taxonomy also is a beneficial step towards certifying or auditing explainable systems.

</details>


### [17] [DCAS: Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding to Internalize Planning across Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06113)
*Kishanthan Thangarajah,Boyuan Chen,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Models fine-tuned on OpenHands overfit to scaffold-specific planning conventions. DCAS enables cross-scaffold evaluation and collection of planning-aware trajectories; targeted fine-tuning on those trajectories improves generalization, and explicit vs implicit planning effects can be separated and quantified.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLI-based software-engineering agents are typically trained using trajectory datasets collected under a single scaffold (OpenHands). Models fine-tuned on these data perform well under the training scaffold but degrade substantially under other scaffolds, while base models do not, implying fine-tuning-induced scaffold overfitting. The authors hypothesize that planning structure—both explicit plans and implicit execution conventions—is a load-bearing scaffold-specific behavior that causes this gap, so they aim to decouple planning from scaffold artifacts and teach planning as a model capability.

Method: They implement DCAS, an interception layer that substitutes backends by routing API calls between scaffolds and models without altering scaffold code. They perform controlled plan-source interventions (swapping or varying where plans come from) to measure planning contribution to performance. Using DCAS, they collect planning-aware trajectories under a scaffold and fine-tune models on a small curated set. They evaluate models across multiple non-training scaffolds to quantify cross-scaffold drops and assess the separability of explicit vs implicit planning in training data.

Result: They propose Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding (DCAS), a backend-substitution interception layer that routes API traffic between any CLI scaffold and any backend model without modifying the scaffold. Using DCAS, they run controlled plan-source interventions that show planning quality drives cross-scaffold drops. Fine-tuning on a small set of DCAS-collected planning-aware trajectories under a single scaffold yields consistent performance gains across non-training scaffolds. They also show the two senses of planning—explicit and implicit—are empirically separable in training data.

Conclusion: Scaffold-specific planning conventions are a primary cause of poor cross-scaffold generalization. Moving planning from a fixed scaffold artifact into a learned model capability—enabled by DCAS and planning-aware data collection—reduces cross-scaffold performance drops. The work demonstrates that small, targeted datasets collected via scaffold-agnostic interception can yield robust gains and that explicit and implicit planning behaviors can be independently trained and analyzed.

Abstract: CLI-based software-engineering agents have matured rapidly, yet the open ecosystem has converged on a single training environment: trajectory datasets used to fine-tune open models are collected almost exclusively under OpenHands. Models fine-tuned on this data score well under OpenHands but degrade substantially when deployed under any non-training scaffold. Untrained base models do not show this divergence, indicating the gap is fine-tuning-induced and tied to the conventions of the training scaffold. We argue that a load-bearing scaffold-specific behavior is planning structure, in two senses this paper distinguishes: explicit planning, a pre-execution plan produced as a first-class artifact, and implicit planning, the structural conventions that shape execution throughout the agent loop. Under this hypothesis, closing the gap requires moving planning from a fixed scaffold artifact to a learned model capability. We introduce Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding (DCAS), a backend-substitution interception layer that routes API traffic between any CLI scaffold and any backend model without modifying the scaffold, enabling cross-scaffold evaluation and planning-aware trajectory collection. Using DCAS, a controlled plan-source intervention confirms planning quality is a high-leverage component, with gains exceeding the cross-scaffold drops we observe. A model fine-tuned on a small set of DCAS-collected planning-aware trajectories under a single scaffold gains consistently across non-training scaffolds, and the two senses of planning are empirically separable in training data.

</details>


### [18] [Learning Globally Reusable Skills for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06153)
*Chen Yang,Jiashuo Tian,Ziqi Wang,Xinyin Liu,Meiru Ye,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出GSE，一种全局化的技能演化框架，通过维护技能关系图（SRG）、基于聚类的技能整合和基于回放的验证来联合优化技能兼容性与泛化能力，从而减少局部过拟合。应用于缺陷相关的两个软件工程任务（揭露缺陷的测试生成与假阳性缺陷报告过滤），在两个现有编程代理（OpenHands、mini-SWE-agent）及工业内部代理上均显著提升精度/召回/F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化技能演化多为局部、串行更新，忽视技能间的相互关系与共享能力，导致更新易过拟合且难以泛化到不同任务或代理。

Method: 维护技能关系图（Skill Relation Graph）以刻画并协同更新技能间的依赖/兼容性；对局部更新做基于聚类的技能整合以抽象通用能力；用回放驱动的验证测试更新以发现并回滚过拟合或行为退化。按迭代流程共同更新技能与关系，评估精度/召回/F1并与既有演化策略比较。

Result: 在测试生成任务上，与现有技术相比，GSE使精度提升6.1%~34.1%、召回提升31.8%~180.0%；在假阳性过滤上，精度提升15.4%~96.4%、召回提升13.1%~19.8%。在内部工业代理部署中，F1提升61.4%。GSE在两个开源代理和工业场景上都达到了最佳指标。

Conclusion: GSE通过显式建模并协同演化技能之间的关系，同时抽象可复用能力并用回放验证防止退化，实现了更具兼容性与泛化性的技能库更新，实验表明其在软件工程任务上优于现有演化技术并具备良好可迁移性。

Abstract: Automated skill evolution enables Large Language Model (LLM) agents to continuously improve without expensive retraining. However, existing approaches typically treat skill evolution as a sequence of local updates, overlooking relationships among skills and often producing overfitted skill updates that fail to generalize across tasks. We propose GSE, a globalized skill evolution framework that jointly optimizes skill compatibility and skill generalization. To preserve consistency across the skill bank, GSE maintains a Skill Relation Graph (SRG) that explicitly models and co-evolves inter-skill relationships. To improve generalization, GSE performs cluster-based skill consolidation to abstract reusable capabilities from local updates and employs replay-driven verification to prevent overfitting and behavioral regressions. We evaluate GSE on two representative software engineering tasks: bug-revealing test generation and false-positive bug report filtering. Across two state-of-the-art coding agents, OpenHands and mini-SWE-agent, GSE consistently achieves the best precision, recall, and F1-score. Compared with existing evolution techniques, GSE improves precision and recall by 6.1%~34.1% and 31.8%~180.0% for test generation, and by 15.4%~96.4% and 13.1%~19.8% for false-positive filtering. Deployment on an internal industrial agent further yields a 61.4% improvement in F1-score, demonstrating the effectiveness and generalizability of GSE for evolving effective skills.

</details>


### [19] [Predicting Agile Success: The Critical Few Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06228)
*Ridewaan Hanslo,Maureen Tanner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 基于实证的PLS-SEM分析，识别出若干对敏捷项目成功有显著影响的关键成功因素，并提出可用于评估项目成功的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管敏捷项目成功率高于传统方法，但总体成功率仍偏低，部分原因是未严格依据关键成功因素（CSF）与现代项目成功标准进行评估；本研究旨在识别影响敏捷项目成功的关键因素并构建测量模型。

Method: 采用实证主义范式；向敏捷从业者收集问卷数据；使用SmartPLS进行偏最小二乘结构方程建模（PLS-SEM）以检验构念之间的路径与显著性。

Result: 研究发现少数关键因素显著促进敏捷项目成功；提出了一个新的评估与度量成功的模型。

Conclusion: 几项关键成功因素对敏捷软件项目成功具有显著影响；采用的模型可用于项目评估，但需在不同情境下进一步验证。

Abstract: While Agile projects are more successful than traditional software development project management approaches, their overall success rate remains relatively low, with a high percentage of projects still deemed challenged or failed. This low project success rate is attributed to projects not being rigorously evaluated against critical success factors (CSF) of Agile projects and contemporary project success criteria. To identify the CSF that contribute to Agile software development project success as perceived by Agile practitioners, this study used a positivist approach to investigate the CSF of Agile software development projects. PLS-SEM with SmartPLS was used to analyse the data and test the hypotheses to identify significant relationships between the constructs and project success criteria. This research found that a few critical factors significantly contribute to Agile project success. The study developed a novel model that can be used to evaluate and measure project success.

</details>


### [20] [Automatic Translation of Unstructured Requirements into Linear Temporal Logic through Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06287)
*Alexandra Newcomb,Omar Ochoa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 评估现代通用大型语言模型(LLM)能否将非结构化自然语言需求直接生成为线性时序逻辑(LTL)公式：使用few-shot提示在15个结构异质的需求上对6个模型各生成5次，共450个候选公式；通过人工语义评估、pass@k({1,3,5})与语法自洽性度量进行性能评估，并结合模型生成的自然语言解释与时间线可视化来考察可理解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证依赖精确定义的规范，但将非结构化自然语言需求转为形式化规格（如LTL）仍然困难且费时；研究旨在判断现成通用LLM能否缩短这一差距，提升对安全/任务关键系统规格化的可行性与效率。

Method: Few-shot提示策略；6个现代LLM；15个结构多样的需求；每个需求-模型组合采5次独立生成——共450个候选LTL；采用人工语义评估、pass@k({1,3,5})指标评估正确率，并测量语法层面的自洽性（不同随机试验间的可重复性）；同时生成自然语言解释并考察与时间线可视化的配合效果。

Result: 实验表明：通用LLM在多数场景下能生成语义上有用的LTL公式，部分模型在pass@k上表现良好；生成结果存在变异性与错误，需要人工审核；配套的自然语言解释与时间线可视化能显著提升非专家的可理解性；总体上LLM可作为半自动化形式化流程的可行前端。

Conclusion: 当下通用LLM在未做任务特定微调的情形下，已能在NL-to-LTL任务上给出具有实际价值的结果，可作为半自动形式化工作流的前端助手，但仍需人工/专家验证以保证严格准确性；可解释性（自然语言说明、时间线可视化）对非专家采纳十分有帮助。

Abstract: Automatically translating unstructured natural language requirements into formal specifications remains a challenge in requirements engineering and formal methods, particularly for safety- and mission-critical systems whose verification depends on mathematically precise specifications. This paper evaluates whether contemporary off-the-shelf Large Language Models (LLMs) can help bridge this gap by generating Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) formulas directly from unstructured requirements. The study examines six modern LLMs using a few-shot prompting strategy on a heterogeneous benchmark of 15 structurally varied requirements. Five independent generations were collected for each requirement-model pair, yielding 450 candidate LTL formulas in total. Performance was assessed through manual semantic evaluation, pass@k for k in {1, 3, 5}, and a self-consistency measure capturing syntactic reproducibility across stochastic trials. The results indicate that current general-purpose LLMs can achieve practically significant performance on the unstructured NL-to-LTL task without task-specific fine-tuning. The study also considers understandability for non-experts by pairing generated formulas with model-produced natural language explanations and discussing the complementary use of timeline-based LTL visualization. The findings suggest that modern LLMs are becoming viable front-end assistants for semi-automated formalization workflows.

</details>

### [21] [Agentic Nesting: A New Methodology for Existing Enterprise Application Integration and Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05159)
*Xi Wang,Kun Li,Xianyao Ling,Gang Yin,Liang Zhang,Jiang Wu,Wenbo Lei,Jun Xu,Annie Wang,Fu Zhang,Weizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“应用即代理”和“会话即集成”的范式：将遗留系统抽象为数字代理，形成分层治理拓扑，由中心编排器协调，旨在降低耦合、提升自治与自然语言交互能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ESB、API网关和RPA在耦合性、运维开销和智能化方面存在局限，需一种更灵活、自然语言驱动且能重用既有资产的集成范式。

Method: 为每个遗留应用提取数字代理代理（natural-language proxy），将代理按嵌套层次组织，中央编排器负责任务分解与调度，前端暴露会话接口；主要依赖语言理解与自动化执行能力实现跨系统协调。

Result: 提出一种名为Agentic Nesting的多智能体协作框架，将现有企业应用封装为自治的AI代理，按层级嵌套组织，通过中央编排器进行任务分解与动态调度，并提供统一的会话接口实现跨应用查询与流程编排。

Conclusion: 方法有望在提升跨异构系统协同与用户可用性方面带来显著优势，但需进一步验证其在可扩展性、事务一致性、安全性与运维成本上的实际表现。

Abstract: Enterprise operations extensively rely on multiple heterogeneous business systems and information applications, which also result in severe data silos and process fragmentation. Enterprises have invested considerable financial and material resources in building these applications, however, effectively leveraging and orchestrating them remains a formidable challenge. Conventional approaches to enterprise application integration, encompassing middleware architectures such as Enterprise Service Bus (ESB), API gateway infrastructures, and Robotic Process Automation (RPA), suffer from inherent limitations like high architectural coupling, escalating operation and maintenance costs, and limited intelligence capabilities. This paper proposes Agentic Nesting, a multi-agent collaboration framework in which existing enterprise applications are encapsulated as autonomous AI agents within a hierarchically nested structure. Rather than flat interconnection, agents are organized into layered stewardship topologies that mirror the compositional complexity of enterprise ecosystems. The framework extracts a digital agent proxy from each legacy application to enable natural-language interaction and autonomous manipulation, coordinates multiple agents through a central orchestrator for task decomposition and dynamic dispatching, and exposes a unified conversational interface for cross-application querying and process orchestration. The main contributions of this paper are the proposition of the "Application-as-Agent" integration paradigm and the "Conversation-as-Integration" interaction philosophy, together with an exploration of the generalization potential of this methodology in scenarios encompassing heterogeneous system coordination, and large-scale data applications.

</details>


### [22] [The Ignition Index: Measuring Global Workspace Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05160)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并验证了一个可量化GWT“点火”预测的指标（Ignition Index），通过sigmoid拟合提取陡峭度β̂，在多模型对比中显示了架构相关差异与统计显著性，但需关注拟合稳健性、控制实验与因果检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将全球工作空间理论（GWT）有关“all-or-none ignition”的预测在神经语言模型中进行可测量、可比较的量化，以便在可解释性框架下检验是否存在突发性全局广播或工作空间样的状态转变。

Method: 对每层在一系列输入信号强度上训练线性探针，获得准确率-信号曲线；用四参数sigmoid拟合并提取陡峭度参数β̂；用打乱标签控制评估选择性；在不同模型/轴/训练步上比较β̂；使用PELT等变点检测识别训练期间的相变；统计检验包括Mann-Whitney U-test、Cohen's d。

Result: 论文提出了Ignition Index (I)，通过对每层线性探针在不同输入信号强度下的准确率拟合四参数sigmoid并提取陡峭度参数β̂来量化“点火式”转变。该指标在11个模型（5个家族）上检验，使用打乱标签控制显示对真实语言结构具有9.6倍选择性（p<0.001）。主要发现包括：前馈Transformer在β̂上显著高于SSM；循环（迭代）轴上表现出更强的点火；在训练中检测到相变先于induction head形成；规模与信号强度未显著相关。代码公开。

Conclusion: Ignition Index为将GWT动力学预测与模型可解释性连接提供了第一个定量工具，结果显示架构（前馈vsSSM、循环）对点火特性有可重复的影响，但若要支持机制性结论仍需更多控制、置信区间、因果干预与替代模型检验。

Abstract: We introduce the Ignition Index (I), a validated scalar metric that operationalizes Global Workspace Theory's (GWT) all-or-none ignition prediction in transformer language models. The metric fits a four-parameter sigmoid to per-layer linear probe accuracy as a function of input signal strength, extracting steepness parameter beta-hat: high values indicate abrupt, ignition-like transitions; low values indicate graded build-up. Across 11 models spanning five architecture families, shuffled-label controls demonstrate 9.6-fold selectivity for genuine linguistic structure over spurious probe capacity (p < 0.001, Mann-Whitney U-test). We find: (1) Feedforward transformers exceed SSMs by 89% in aggregate beta-hat (p < 1e-13, Cohen's d = 0.52), with Mamba exhibiting near-linear profiles consistent with absent global broadcast. (2) Huginn-3.5B exhibits 2.12-fold higher ignition along its iteration axis than its depth axis, demonstrating that recurrent architectures manifest workspace-like transitions along the recurrence dimension. (3) Pythia-410M shows a PELT-detected phase transition at training step 256 (+67%), preceding induction-head formation. (4) Hypotheses linking ignition to model scale and signal strength were not confirmed, suggesting transformer architectures may saturate available ignition mechanisms. The Ignition Index provides the first validated quantitative bridge between GWT's dynamical predictions and mechanistic interpretability, with 9.6-fold measurement selectivity and architecture-level discriminability not previously characterized in the scaling literature. Code: https://github.com/saman-rahbar/ignition-index

</details>


### [23] [Woodpecker Distillation: Weak Models Diagnose Reasoning Bugs in Strong Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05168)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Yang Li,Deguo Xia,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM reasoning errors often are local and fixable via short weak-model patches; distilling corrective future-token distributions (Woodpecker Distillation) into strong models improves math reasoning accuracy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Many failures in LLM reasoning stem from local reasoning bugs rather than global incompetence; these bugs can be fixed by short patches from weak probes that redirect the strong model's reasoning trajectory.

Method: Analyze abstract

Result: Woodpecker Distillation, a training framework, contrasts successful vs unsuccessful weak-model patches to form a corrective teacher distribution over future tokens, and distills this into the strong model, improving performance on math benchmarks over imitation baselines.

Conclusion: Learning from contrastive local interventions (not direct imitation) helps strong models internalize corrective trajectory changes and boosts reasoning performance.

Abstract: Large language models often fail on reasoning tasks despite possessing the capability to solve them. We argue that many such failures arise from localized reasoning bugs in intermediate steps rather than from global incompetence. We show that these bugs are frequently repairable: inserting a short patch generated by a weak probe model after the same strong-model reasoning prefix can redirect the trajectory toward a correct solution.
  However, this corrective effect is not reliably internalized by directly fine-tuning on weak patches or repaired trajectories, suggesting that the useful signal lies not in the intervention text itself, but in how it reshapes the model's future reasoning distribution. We therefore propose Woodpecker Distillation, a weak-to-strong training framework that learns from contrastive local interventions. Our method contrasts successful and unsuccessful weak-model patches at the same prefix, constructs a corrective teacher distribution from their induced future token predictions, and distills this signal into the strong model.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Woodpecker Distillation consistently improves strong-model performance and outperforms direct imitation baselines.

</details>


### [24] [From Continuous Predictors to Clinical Thresholds: Early Evidence on Performance Trade-offs of Guideline-Based Categorisation for Ischaemic Stroke Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05203)
*Esra Zihni,Katryna Cisek,Hamzah Ziadeh,Hendrik Knoche,Robert Mikulik,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用治疗特异、指南对齐的阈值对连续变量进行完全类别化，在大多数队列中不降低GBM预测性能且保持特征重要性排序，说明这种设计可提高临床可解释性而通常不牺牲准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床采用受限的主要原因之一是模型解释与临床推理不一致；临床医生要求使用符合指南的临界值（cut-offs），以便模型输出更易理解和可信。研究问题是：将连续预测变量替换为临床指南对齐的类别编码，是否在不损失性能的前提下提高可解释性与可用性？

Method: 在多中心欧洲登记数据库上，将数据按三类治疗队列分层；构建并比较标准（使用连续预测变量）与完全类别化（将连续预测变量按中风指南对不同治疗组设定的阈值离散化）的梯度提升模型（GBM）。通过性能度量与统计检验比较两类模型，并计算全局特征重要性以评估层次一致性。

Result: 在两名治疗队列中，完全类别化模型与连续模型在预测90天结局上统计上不可区分；在第三队列出现显著的预测性能下降。尽管个别性能差异存在，所有组的全局特征重要性排序保持一致，表明类别化未改变主要预后因子的相对重要性。

Conclusion: 基于指南的类别化对中风预后模型是可行的：在两个治疗队列中，完全类别化的梯度提升模型与连续变量模型在预测准确性上无显著差异；在一个队列中性能显著下降。类别化并未改变全局特征重要性排序，表明保留了核心预后因素的层次结构。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve strong predictive accuracy for 90-day outcome prediction in acute ischaemic stroke, yet clinical adoption is limited by the misalignment of model explanations with clinicians' reasoning. Motivated by a clinician user study calling for clinical guideline-aligned cut-offs, we ask whether continuous predictors can be replaced by clinically informed categorical encodings without sacrificing performance. On a multi-centre European registry stratified into three treatment cohorts, we compare standard and fully categorised gradient-boosted models, the latter using stroke guideline-aligned, treatment-specific thresholds. The fully categorised models are statistically indistinguishable from their continuous counterparts in two of the treatment cohorts, with a significant drop in predictive accuracy in one cohort. Global feature importance rankings remain consistent, suggesting that discretising continuous predictors into guideline-based categories preserves the core hierarchy of prognostic factors across all treatment groups. Guideline-based categorisation is thus a viable design choice for stroke-outcome models.

</details>


### [25] [SkillTrace: Multi-Trace Provenance Auditing for LLM-Agent Skill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05204)
*Jialuo Chen,Minghe Wang,Lingqi Jiang,Jianan Ma,Xinhao Deng,Xiaohu Du,Ruixiao Lin,Yunhao Feng,Linkang Du,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对可复用、多模态的LLM-agent技能，SKILLTRACE通过三条溯源轨迹与SOG表示实现更细粒度的复用证据检出，相比单一代码/包相似度检测更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着技能成为市场化商品，技能复用的证据分散在文本、实现与运行结构中，传统单模态克隆检测无法捕捉仅保留部分信息的复用，需要一种多模态可溯源审计方法。

Method: 抽取三类轨迹：1) Expression（作者文本/说明） 2) Implementation（实现片段/代码） 3) Operational（流程、激活与资源流），将Operational建模为技能操作图（SOG）；使用LLM仅在摄取时辅助提取Operational轨迹，审计时比较缓存轨迹并与同功能严格负例校准，返回支持复用判定的轨迹证据。

Result: SKILLTRACE提出了一套用于LLM-agent技能复用审计的多轨迹可溯源框架，通过抽取Expression、Implementation、Operational三条溯源轨迹，并将Operational轨迹表示为技能操作图（SOG），结合一次性的LLM辅助提取与审计时的确定性比对、与同功能严格负例校准，能够在基准与大规模野外审计中显著识别被变换后的复用。基准结果AUROC=0.938、F1=0.898，36k技能的野外审计证明了可操作性。

Conclusion: 多轨迹审计（尤其引入Operational/SOG）能补足传统克隆检测的盲点，方法在构造的基准和大规模审计中表现良好，但需要更多关于SOG设计、LLM辅助提取鲁棒性、负例构造与误报分析的细节以评估普适性与可复现性。

Abstract: LLM-agent ecosystems are rapidly growing around reusable skills: mixed-modality packages of metadata, natural-language instructions, code, tools, references, and operational workflows. As skills become marketplace artifacts, auditing their reuse is no longer the same problem as ordinary code clone detection. Existing detectors target single-modality source code or whole-package similarity, yet skill reuse evidence is distributed across authored text, implementation fragments, and operational structure. As a result, they can miss reuse that preserves only one part of a skill. We present SKILLTRACE, a multi-trace provenance auditing framework for LLM-agent skill reuse. SKILLTRACE extracts three provenance traces: Expression, Implementation, and Operational. It represents the Operational Trace as a Skill Operational Graph (SOG) that captures activation, procedure, and resource-flow structure. An LLM assists only the Operational-trace extraction, once at ingestion; at audit time SKILLTRACE compares cached traces deterministically, calibrates each trace against same-function strict negatives, and reports which trace supports a reuse decision. On SKILLTRACE-BENCH, with 820 transformed reuse positives over 100 marketplace anchors and 751 negative controls, SKILLTRACE achieves AUROC 0.938 and F1 0.898. A 36,446-skill wild audit further shows that trace-attributed evidence surfaces actionable reuse review queues beyond repository-level baselines.

</details>


### [26] [Abstract Event Causal Rules: Induction and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05205)
*Ziwei Zheng,Peiqiong Chen,Bang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将实例级因果对抽象为关系级AECR并以CACI提取规则、AR-GCAE注入规则，从而显著提高事件因果推理和罕见/未见事件的预测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件因果知识多为实例层面的因果对，难以对低频长尾和未见事件组合进行泛化，限制了事件智能分析系统在预警、决策支持和叙事理解中的表现。

Method: 定义AECR抽象范式；构建多智能体CACI流水线并用相似度约束聚类去噪与归纳AECR；建立两个AECR知识库；设计AR-GCAE，通过规则引导注意力层和门控表示融合把检索到的AECRs注入CGEP基准任务，进行定量评估。

Result: 提出了Abstract Event Causal Rule (AECR)——一种将具体因果对抽象为关系层次通用因果逻辑的新范式；设计了多智能体Concrete-to-Abstract Causal Induction (CACI)系统并结合相似度约束聚类从噪声数据中提取可信AECRs，构建了两个完整的AECR知识库；并通过Abstract Rule-Guided Causal Attention Encoder (AR-GCAE)将AECR注入因果图事件预测任务CGEP，实验证明在罕见与未见样本上显著提升了泛化与预测性能。

Conclusion: 抽象化的因果规则能保留内在因果关系并显著增强模型在长尾与未见事件上的泛化能力；所构建的AECR知识库和规则引导的编码器对事件预测任务带来一致且显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Event-centric intelligent analytical systems heavily depend on explicit causal event knowledge for risk early warning, decision-making support and narrative comprehension. Nevertheless, existing instance-level causal pairs suffer severe generalization deficits on low-frequency long-tail and unseen event combinations. To address this limitation, this work proposes Abstract Event Causal Rule (AECR), a novel relation-level causal abstraction paradigm that transforms concrete cause-effect pairs into generalized abstract causal logic while retaining their intrinsic causal relationships. We design a multi-agent Concrete-to-Abstract Causal Induction (CACI) system coupled with similarity-constrained clustering to distill trustworthy AECRs from noisy raw causal data, based on which two complete AECR knowledge bases are built. To validate the practical utility of abstract causal knowledge, we propose an Abstract Rule-Guided Causal Attention Encoder (AR-GCAE), which injects the retrieved AECRs into the causality Graph Event Prediction (CGEP) benchmark task via rule-guided attention layers and gated representation fusion. Quantitative experimental results reveal that applying AECRs substantially strengthens the generalization capacity of event causal reasoning and brings consistent performance improvements to event prediction, with the most prominent gains observed on rare and unseen event samples.

</details>


### [27] [Otter: A Time-Aware, History-Conditioned Human Chess AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05206)
*Tarun Kumar S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将走子序列和时钟信息作为条件输入的轻量级模型，在人类落子预测上优于位置孤立模型，且使用更小模型与更少数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人类落子预测多把每个局面孤立建模，忽略了开局选择、对局倾向和钟面压力的序列与时间因素。目标是用更小的模型通过条件化历史与时间信息更准确地拟合人类落子行为。

Method: 模型为15.3M参数，输入包括当前棋盘、最近20步走子编码（捕捉开局偏好、位置漂移、局内行为倾向）与时间控制模块（基于剩余思考时间调整预测）；在117M盘Lichess rapid游戏上训练，覆盖6.1B个位置，单T4 GPU训练30天；评估采用top-1和top-5落子预测精度，并按Elo段统计。

Result: Otter是一个15.3M参数的人类棋类预测模型，通过将棋局视为带时间感知的序列过程（使用最近20步的走子历史编码器和考虑剩余时间的时间控制模块）来预测人类落子。模型在117M盘Lichess rapid比赛（共6.1B个棋局位置）上训练，单卡T4训练30天，实现55.23% top-1 和90.95% top-5精度，超越了参数更多且训练数据更多的Maia 2；在按Elo分组的11个区间中，1900–1999分段达到最高57.38%。代码与训练日志公开。

Conclusion: 时间感知的序列建模（20步历史 + 时钟压力）能显著提升人类落子预测准确率，且在资源受限条件下能比更大模型取得更好或可比的表现；公开数据与日志利于复现与进一步研究。

Abstract: Otter is a 15.3M-parameter human chess AI that predicts human move selection by modeling play as a time-aware, sequential process rather than treating each position in isolation. It combines two conditioning signals: (1) a move history encoder that conditions predictions on the last 20 moves, capturing opening preferences, positional drift, and intra-game behavioral tendencies; and (2) a time control module that modulates predictions based on clock pressure. Otter is trained on 6.1 billion positions from 117 million Lichess rapid games over 30 days on a single T4 GPU.
  Otter achieves 55.23% top-1 and 90.95% top-5 move-prediction accuracy, surpassing the prior state-of-the-art human chess model, Maia 2, with far fewer parameters and less training data. Across 11 Elo brackets (<1100 to >=2000), accuracy peaks at 57.38% in the 1900-1999 bracket. These results show that modeling chess as a time-aware, sequential activity yields more human-accurate move prediction than position-only approaches, using a smaller model. Code, trained models, and complete training logs are publicly released.

</details>


### [28] [SearchAuditor: Auditing and Attributing Failures in Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05212)
*Zhixiang Liang,Yifei Liu,Yidan Huang,Haozhe Zhao,Beichen Huang,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan,Qiong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Built a large, expert-annotated benchmark of long failed deep-search trajectories and a multi-perspective LLM auditing framework; empirical results show auditing improves recovery but overall pass rates remain low, highlighting persistent brittleness.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deep search agents interact with the web over long horizons and are prone to brittle failures where small reasoning errors cascade into incorrect outputs; manual diagnosis of such long, noisy traces is impractical. The work aims to enable LLM-based auditors to localize, attribute, and repair failures to reduce human burden.

Method: Collected 1,243 failed runs from eight open-weight models across five deep-search tasks; expert-annotated critical error steps, root causes, and repairs with grading rubrics. Designed SearchAuditor to perform evidence-grounded adjudication across multiple perspectives to pinpoint and fix failures, and evaluated against baseline auditors using frontier LLMs (e.g., GPT-5.5) measuring end-to-end pass rates and recovery after repairs.

Result: Introduces SearchAuditBench: 1,243 failed trajectories (avg 73.1 messages, 65.1K tokens) with expert annotations (critical error step, root cause, reference repair). Proposes SearchAuditor, a multi-perspective, evidence-grounded auditing pipeline that localizes, attributes, and repairs failures. Empirically, even strong baselines with GPT-5.5 reach only 26.6% end-to-end pass; SearchAuditor improves this to 32.3% and its repairs help agents recover.

Conclusion: SearchAuditBench enables systematic evaluation of failure diagnosis for deep-search agents; SearchAuditor outperforms baselines at localizing, attributing, and repairing failures, raising end-to-end pass rates but leaving substantial headroom for future improvement.

Abstract: Deep search agents tackle challenging questions through long-horizon web interactions, a process that is both complex and fragile: small reasoning errors may propagate through long, noisy trajectories into fluent but incorrect answers. Diagnosing such failures is difficult, requiring the manual inspection of extremely long execution traces, which could be beyond human capacity. We therefore introduce SearchAuditBench, a benchmark that evaluates whether LLM auditors can localize, attribute, and repair these failures, thereby reducing the human burden. SearchAuditBench comprises 1,243 failed trajectories, averaging 73.1 messages and 65.1K tokens, collected from eight open-weight models on five deep-search benchmarks, each expert-annotated with the critical error step, a search-specific root cause, and a reference repair with grading rubrics. We further propose SearchAuditor, a multi-perspective auditing framework that effectively localizes, attributes, and repairs search-agent failures through evidence-grounded adjudication. Experimental results show that even the strongest baseline, when powered by a frontier model like GPT-5.5, attains only a 26.6% end-to-end pass rate. In contrast, our SearchAuditor consistently outperforms all baselines across different frontier models, achieving an end-to-end pass rate of 32.3%, and resuming failed runs with its repairs enables agents to better recover from errors.

</details>


### [29] [PD-GS: Phoneme-Driven 3DGS for Audio-Driven Talking Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05218)
*Ao Fu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduce phoneme-conditioning and an adaptive fusion gate into 3D Gaussian Splatting to fix over-smoothed mouth motion; trained on monocular video, PD-GS reduces closure violations and improves lip geometry (LMD 2.66).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Continuous acoustic embeddings and regression objectives bias mouth predictions toward averaged configurations, causing over-smoothed motion and ‘leaky mouth’ (closure violations). Self-supervised speech encoders lack explicit frame-aligned linguistic targets to disambiguate short closure events.

Method: Augment a 3D Gaussian Splatting talker with phoneme tokens from ASR + forced alignment; introduce a Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM) that fuses continuous audio context and discrete phoneme embeddings via a learned gating mechanism; train from monocular video using image reconstruction loss and lip landmark supervision.

Result: On HDTF, PD-GS attains state-of-the-art lip geometry (LMD 2.66) among compared baselines and qualitatively reduces bilabial and other closure violations, yielding more faithful neural avatars.

Conclusion: PD-GS improves lip articulation in 3D Gaussian Splatting-based talking heads by augmenting continuous audio embeddings with time-aligned phoneme tokens and an adaptive fusion module, reducing closure violations and producing more linguistically faithful mouth motion.

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables fast, photorealistic talking-head rendering, yet accurate lip articulation remains elusive: mouth motion is often over-smoothed and may violate hard articulatory constraints such as bilabial closures, producing the notorious ``leaky mouth'' artifact. A key difficulty is that brief, discrete articulatory events are inferred from a continuous acoustic embedding under a regression objective, which biases predictions toward averaged mouth configurations. While modern self-supervised speech encoders provide rich prosodic and phonetic cues, they do not provide an explicit, frame-aligned linguistic target that reliably disambiguates closure-level events. We propose \textbf{Phoneme-Driven Gaussian Splatting (PD-GS)}, which augments a 3DGS talker with time-aligned phoneme tokens obtained from an automatic ASR and forced-alignment pipeline. Our core component, the \textbf{Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM)}, adaptively fuses continuous audio context with discrete phoneme embeddings through a learned gate, allowing the model to preserve smooth audio-driven dynamics while strengthening phoneme guidance on articulation-critical segments. PD-GS is trained purely from monocular video using image reconstruction and lip landmark supervision. On HDTF, PD-GS achieves the best lip geometry among the compared baselines (LMD 2.66) and qualitatively reduces closure violations in challenging phoneme sequences, yielding more linguistically faithful neural avatars.

</details>


### [30] [When Privileged Guidance Misaligns: State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05219)
*Junzhuo Liu,Weiwei Li,Jun Ling,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在交互式环境中，通过在每步验证学生当前状态是否与参考轨迹支持的状态匹配并仅在匹配时用构建的状态条件化教师上下文进行蒸馏，SMRC-SD避免了状态-参考不匹配，从而显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Privileged on-policy distillation gives dense supervision by letting a teacher rescore the student’s response each turn using training-only references (successful trajectories). In interactive environments the student’s actions change execution state so the student's rollout may reach states not covered by the reference, creating state–reference mismatch and making unconditional privileged distillation unreliable.

Method: SMRC-SD在每个回合：1) 验证学生执行状态是否与参考轨迹中的某一受支持状态匹配（路由/筛选），2) 若匹配则构造基于该状态的教师上下文（将成功轨迹切片或条件化以贴合当前状态），并对学生进行蒸馏；否则跳过该回合蒸馏。通过在ALFWorld和WebShop上与基线和消融研究比较，证明选择性路由与上下文条件化各自均对性能提升有贡献。

Result: Proposes SMRC-SD (State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation). At every turn it checks whether the student’s current execution state matches some state on a successful reference trajectory; only when matched is distillation applied, and supervision is grounded by constructing state-conditioned teacher context. Across ALFWorld and WebShop, SMRC-SD outperforms unconditional full-path distillation: e.g., with Qwen3-1.7B, success improves from 0.746→0.865 (ALFWorld) and 0.574→0.693 (WebShop). Ablations show both routing and contextualized teacher context contribute to gains; code released.

Conclusion: 有选择地在局部兼容的时刻进行蒸馏、并用与当前状态对齐的教师上下文来提供监督，能有效缓解状态–参考不匹配问题，显著提高多回合代理的成功率。

Abstract: Privileged on-policy distillation provides dense supervision for multi-turn agents by allowing a synchronized teacher to re-score the student's response at every turn with access to training-only references, such as successful trajectories. In interactive environments, however, the student's preceding actions continually change the execution state. As the student takes different actions or completes subgoals in a different order, its rollout may reach states not covered by the reference, making the reference an unreliable source of guidance for the state actually reached. Applying privileged distillation indiscriminately therefore creates state--reference mismatch. This mismatch motivates a central objective: providing privileged reference guidance that remains compatible with the student's current execution state. We introduce State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation (SMRC-SD), which explicitly determines when and how a privileged trajectory should guide an on-policy student. At each turn, SMRC-SD verifies whether the student's current execution state matches a supported state along the reference trajectory. Distillation is applied only at matched states, filtering out turns for which the reference lacks locally compatible guidance. For each matched state, SMRC-SD further constructs state-conditioned teacher context from the successful trajectory, grounding supervision in the state actually reached. Across ALFWorld and WebShop, SMRC-SD consistently outperforms unconditional successful full-path distillation. With Qwen3-1.7B, it improves task success from $0.746$ to $0.865$ on ALFWorld and from $0.574$ to $0.693$ on WebShop. Controlled routing and context ablations support both selecting locally supported turns and constructing state-compatible teacher context as contributors to these gains. Code is available at https://github.com/liujunzhuo/SMRC-SD.

</details>


### [31] [Small Foundation Models of Human Cognition and Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05224)
*Nick Oh,Fernand Gobet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在心理行为数据上微调后，中等规模（≈0.6–1B）模型在同分布被试预测上已接近大型模型，但大型模型在未知任务结构上泛化更好；模型主要依赖刺激与反馈信息，选择历史不足以解释其表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验在大规模人类行为数据上微调的语言模型是否学到了任务结构的心理机制，还是仅依赖统计捷径；同时探索所需的模型规模与泛化能力的关系。

Method: 数据与训练：使用Psych-101（10.7M trial-level choices, 160 experiments），训练14个不同参数规模与架构的模型。评估分为同分布（held-out participants）与跨结构（out-of-distribution）测试。诊断：对27个实验逐步屏蔽四个提示通道（任务说明、刺激、反馈、选择历史），并对试次顺序进行置换，量化性能降幅（报告为“已学信息”损失75.7%等指标）。

Result: 训练与发现：作者在Psych-101数据集（10.7M 次试验选择，160 个实验）上微调了14个模型（从135M到14B参数，四种架构），发现对训练集/相似任务（in-distribution）规模影响很小：0.6B–1B参数即可匹配70B基线；对新任务结构（out-of-distribution）则更依赖规模，较大模型具备更强泛化能力。诊断实验通过逐步屏蔽四种提示通道（任务说明、刺激内容、反馈、选择历史）和打乱试次顺序来判定模型利用的信息。屏蔽刺激与反馈摧毁了75.7%的已学信息并使表现降到低于机会水平，说明仅靠选择历史无法解释性能；试次置换显示对独立试次任务不敏感，但对具有响应依赖顺序的任务敏感。总体结论是：小规模经过认知行为微调的模型可作为心理学实验的噪声天花板估计器，但其适用范围受限于训练中见过的范式。

Conclusion: 1) 在同分布被试上，模型性能存在上限，较大参数量回报递减；2) 在跨任务/结构泛化上，大模型明显有利；3) 刺激与反馈内容是模型预测行为的主要信息来源，选择历史不是充分原因；4) 小规模认知微调模型可作为某类实验的噪声天花板估计器，但不应超出其训练范式外推。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned on human behavioural data have emerged as general-purpose cognitive proxies, but the scale this requires, and whether these models process task structure or exploit statistical shortcuts, remain open questions. We train fourteen models from 135M to 14B parameters across four architecture families on Psych-101, a dataset of 10.7 million trial-level choices from 160 experiments. In-distribution, scale barely matters. The models fall within a narrow band, as though against a ceiling, and 0.6B to 1B parameters suffice to match a 70B baseline on held-out participants. Out-of-distribution, that band opens into a markedly steeper scaling gradient, with larger models clearly advantaged in generalisation to novel task structure. To determine what information these models use, we run two diagnostics. We progressively strip four prompt channels -- task instructions, experimental stimuli, outcome feedback, and choice history -- across 27 experiments, and permute trial order. Masking the content of stimuli and feedback destroys 75.7% of learned information and pushes models below chance, demonstrating that choice history alone does not account for performance. Permutation reveals invariance on tasks with independent trials but sensitivity where trial order is determined by prior responses. Small cognitively fine-tuned models therefore show promise as noise ceiling estimators for psychological experiments, though their scope remains bounded by the paradigms seen in training.

</details>


### [32] [Project2Task: Graph-Guided Project-Level Planning for Autonomous Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05225)
*Huirui Xu,Runtao Xu,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Project2Task，通过图引导与块模型选择将研究项目分解为依赖感知、可执行的任务契约，从而减少冗余并显著提升任务质量与下游执行表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单任务系统把研究项目视为一个超大任务或产出平铺直叙、模糊/重叠的任务集合，缺乏边界、执行顺序和依赖管理，导致协调困难和结果碎片化。

Method: 提出基于图的项目级规划层：将候选贡献表示为“创新原子”，构建有向血统图；采用轻量伯努利块模型（Bernoulli block-model）目标在横向、纵向和混合组合间选择分解策略；生成带有明确贡献归属和执行契约（目标、输入、预期制品、评估需求、边界、依赖和执行顺序）的有界任务。

Result: 在10个项目简报（约30个任务）基准上，基于手稿的组合评估得分平均7.15，明显高于简报基线（4.58）和仅主题设定（5.31）；将其任务契约与AutoResearchClaw集成可把下游任务准确率从0.536提高到0.759。

Conclusion: Project2Task 有效将研究项目分解为有界、非冗余、依赖感知的任务组合，从而提升项目级别的一致性和下游执行准确率。

Abstract: Research agents can increasingly search literature, propose hypotheses, generate code, run experiments, and draft manuscripts from a single topic. However, a research project is not merely a larger task: it is a long-horizon agenda that must be advanced through multiple bounded tasks with distinct but related objectives, parallel alternatives, and dependency-aware sequences. Existing single-task systems often treat the project as one oversized task, produce a flat set of vague or overlapping tasks, or leave task boundaries and execution order to manual coordination. We introduce Project2Task, a graph-guided project-level planning layer for autonomous research. Given a project brief, it represents candidate contributions as innovation atoms and organizes them in a directed lineage graph. A lightweight Bernoulli block-model objective selects among horizontal, vertical, and hybrid portfolio decompositions.
